# Match 9057336754: Luna — loss

Perspective: account **467233306**, Dire. Duration: 56:50. K/D/A: 12/8/22.

[Original cached OpenDota JSON](../matches/9057336754.json). Evidence uses JSON pointers into this file; hero names use [cached constants](../constants/heroes.json).

## Evidence and limits

This is a chronological reconstruction of recorded events, not a replay video review. No intent, vision, map awareness, decision reason, or mistake is inferred. Missing timestamps are omitted and reported; empty logs mean no recorded entries, not proof no event occurred. Kill and death logs are merged only for exact timestamp/killer/victim matches; unmatched records remain separate observations. Purchase-log entries are not proof of delivery, use, or assembled-item completion. Detected teamfights are OpenDota intervals; listed activity does not establish exact positioning or a teamfight winner.

Team advantage is from **your side**: positive favors your team; Dire reverses the original Radiant sign. Gold uses `radiant_gold_adv` and XP uses `radiant_xp_adv`, aligned only when arrays match recorded player `times`. No team net-worth series is fabricated from these fields. Event context targets 60 seconds before the start and 60 seconds after the end, allowing one extra minute to find a real sample. Every displayed sample keeps its original time, Radiant value, and source pointers; missing samples are unavailable, with no interpolation.

## Potential turning points

Rule: your team loses at least 2,500 in recorded gold advantage over a 4–5 minute observed window, then loses a tower, barracks, or Ancient within 120 seconds of the ending sample. Only pre-objective samples qualify. This is a transparent candidate-selection heuristic; overlapping findings can describe the same broader swing.
